# Verification records

One line per trial on real binaries, oldest first: a record's own line, then one for each dated entry appended to it. The full JSON records these lines came from were kept in the repository until September 21, 2026; each line links to its record as frozen at `5656fe8f` (the last commit that carried them), and `git log -- docs/verification` has their history. A new trial is one new line here, with its source commit and where its evidence is; a new record is not added as a file.

An October 1 retention audit found that the private `/tmp` evidence files had expired. Git commits and hosted CI survived. Recovered CI logs/artifacts and Oracle reports now live outside temporary storage in private `AgentDocker-evidence/resume-20261001`; older local-only raw evidence is unavailable unless recovered or repeated. Historical rows record their original observations, not fresh acceptance of a later source.
